At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [241] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSWIP 为 DeepProbLog 引入单一世界反事实语义，通过神经物化将神经谓词归约为普通 ProbLog 选择，利用 SWIP 和加权模型计数在单一变换程序上精确计算反事实，并在 MPI3D 和 SUMO HOV 实验中验证了推断正确性与加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号系统（如 DeepProbLog）的推理仅支持关联性，缺乏因果语义下的干预与反事实推理能力，因此需要一种能结合神经感知与概率逻辑因果反事实的形式化框架。

Method: 提出 DeepSWIP：将 DeepProbLog 程序中的固定上下文神经谓词通过神经物化归约为普通 ProbLog 选择，再应用单一世界干预程序（SWIP）进行干预建模，最终利用加权模型计数（WMC）在单一变换程序上计算反事实查询。理论分析基于有限基化与唯一支持模型假设，证明 DeepSWIP 相对物化 FCM 是精确的。同时利用 ProbLog 条件概率的商-WMC 形式解析神经概率的活跃性、干预清洗、校准敏感性与稀有证据不稳定性。

Result: 在 MPI3D 上，DeepSWIP 的变换与 DeepTwin 构造在 12,000 个查询上结果一致，且因避免 Twin 的内生重复获得 2.14 倍推理加速。SUMO HOV 实验表明神经校准退化会偏置插件估计，而正确限定随机策略的 AIPW 估计器可消除总体均值和 ATE 估计量中的大部分一阶偏误。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP 成功将 SWIP 反事实语义扩展到神经概率逻辑程序，在保持精确性的同时实现了显著推理加速，并揭示了神经感知组件在校准退化时对因果估计的偏置影响及其校正策略。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [242] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LedgerAgent 提出一种推理时方法，通过独立的"账本"显式维护任务状态，并在工具调用前进行策略约束检查，从而减少客服场景中工具调用智能体的状态管理与策略违反错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准工具调用智能体将观察、工具返回和策略指令全部放入提示中，导致任务状态隐式管理，容易产生两类失败模式：(1) 基于过时或错误信息做出决策；(2) 语法正确的工具调用却违反依赖当前任务状态的领域策略。

Method: 提出 LedgerAgent，在推理时将观察到的任务状态维护在独立账本中，并将状态渲染到提示中；同时利用账本在环境变更类工具调用执行前进行状态依赖的策略约束检查，拦截违规调用。

Result: 在四个客服领域和一个混合开源与闭源模型评测面板上，LedgerAgent 在平均 pass^k 指标上优于标准基于提示的工具调用方法，在更严格的多轮一致性指标下增益最为显著。

Conclusion: 显式状态管理与策略检查相结合的 LedgerAgent 方法能有效提升工具调用智能体在需遵守领域策略的多轮客服场景中的可靠性与合规性。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [243] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将DAAM（扩散注意力归因）框架适配到语音领域，提出跨注意力归因方法用于语音扩散模型，以CapSpeech-TTS为对象，分析风格描述文本中每个词符如何影响生成语音的声学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格描述驱动的TTS系统中，自然语言如何逐词影响声学输出尚不明确。理解这一机制对于诊断失败模式、提升表达性TTS的可控性至关重要。

Method: 提出面向语音扩散模型的跨注意力归因方法：将DAAM框架首次迁移至语音领域，在CapSpeech-TTS的25层网络和24个ODE步骤上提取逐词符热力图。分析3600组（120条风格描述 × 30条文本转写）样本，系统考察风格词符对波形生成的影响。

Result: (1) 风格词符的时间方差低于内容/功能词符，证实其全局条件作用；(2) 风格注意力与F0和能量相关；(3) 风格条件作用在早期ODE步骤和深层网络中达到峰值；(4) 注意力熵在第17层达到最小，与风格重要性峰值同时出现，表明网络在风格最关键阶段具有最大选择性。

Conclusion: 这是首个关于自然语言如何影响语音扩散模型中跨注意力机制的研究，揭示了风格条件的时序与层级动态规律，为改进表达性TTS的可控性提供了可解释性基础。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [244] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文研究了混合专家模型在分布偏移下的校准行为，分析了硬路由与软路由机制中专家级校准对全局校准的充分性条件，并提出对抗重加权方法来改善软路由模型在分布偏移下的准确率-校准权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型虽然实证上展现出校准带来的性能提升，但校准在何种条件下对MoE有益尚未被充分理解，尤其是在分布偏移场景下路由机制与专家级校准的交互关系不明确。

Method: 作者首先理论分析了硬路由与软路由MoE在分布偏移下的校准性质，证明专家校准对硬路由模型全局校准的充分性，及其对软路由模型的不充分性；进而提出一种对抗重加权方法，在分布偏移下惩罚路由聚合后的校准误差。

Result: 对抗重加权方法在多个模型类别、预测任务和分布偏移场景下，均能在平均性能和困难数据子集上改善准确率-校准的权衡。

Conclusion: 专家级校准仅对硬路由MoE在分布偏移下保证全局校准是充分的，而对软路由则不充分；所提出的对抗重加权方法能有效提升软路由模型在分布偏移下的校准性与准确性权衡。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [The Representational Limit of Scalar Interactions: An Interventional Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19410)
*Potito Aghilar,Sabino Roccotelli,Stanislao Fidanza,Vito Walter Anelli,Sebastiano Stramaglia,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了成对交互得分的根本缺陷——唯一性(U)、冗余性(R)和协同性(S)被混为一谈，并提出了Stochastic Hi-Fi方法，通过干预式掩码推理将三者分解，在表格数据、大语言模型电路和医学影像上均取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征交互归因方法存在根本性缺陷：成对交互得分将唯一性（U）、冗余性（R）和协同性（S）三种不同的信息机制混淆为单一标量。作者通过最小3路XOR结构因果模型严格证明，忠实性指标（如Shapley-Taylor）对每对特征返回零值，而投影性指标（如Shapley Interaction）则将三阶效应分散到成对标量中，导致三种机制无法区分。

Method: 提出Stochastic Hi-Fi（Stochastic Hierarchical Feature Interaction）：一种后验、无需重训练的预测可分解性方法。核心是通过干预式掩码推理（interventional masked inference）对每个特征估计U/R/S轮廓。技术要点包括：(1)精确的干预语义；(2)有限样本蒙特卡洛边界；(3)耦合菱形采样实现严格方差缩减；(4)有限词汇表上的均匀收敛保证。

Result: 在表格型结构因果模型（SCM）上，Stochastic Hi-Fi恢复的结构被标量基线方法遗漏，交互幅度恢复比率最高达411倍。在GPT-2的IOI（Indirect Object Identification）电路中，成功分离了冗余头和协同头。在NIH ChestX-ray14数据集上，Pointing Game指标与GradCAM持平，Deletion AUC显著提升。

Conclusion: 成对交互得分的U/R/S混淆是一个被长期忽视的根本性问题。Stochastic Hi-Fi通过分解三类信息机制，在可解释性、忠实性和实际下游任务中均展现出优于传统标量方法的性能，为特征交互归因提供了更细粒度和更忠实的新范式。

Abstract: Signed pairwise interaction scores fundamentally conflate uniqueness (U), redundancy (R), and synergy (S). We prove this on a minimal 3-way XOR structural causal model: faithful indices such as Shapley-Taylor return zero per pair, whereas projective indices such as Shapley Interaction spread the third-order effect into pair scalars that conflate the three mechanisms. We introduce Stochastic Hi-Fi, a post-hoc, retraining-free predictability decomposition that estimates per-feature U/R/S profiles by interventional masked inference. The estimator provides exact interventional semantics, finite-sample Monte Carlo bounds, strict variance reduction from coupled diamond sampling, and uniform finite-vocabulary convergence. Across tabular SCMs, Stochastic Hi-Fi recovers structure missed by scalar baselines (up to 411x larger interaction-magnitude recovery ratios). It also separates redundant and synergistic heads in the GPT-2 IOI circuit. On NIH ChestX-ray14, Stochastic Hi-Fi matches GradCAM on Pointing Game and improves substantially on Deletion AUC.

</details>


### [246] [A Solver-Free Training Method for Predict-then-Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19587)
*Beichen Wan,Mo Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于测度变换原理的决策聚焦学习框架，通过构建无需优化求解器参与的替代损失函数，实现可扩展的predict-then-optimize训练，在保持决策质量的同时将训练时间降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有predict-then-optimize方法通过平滑化处理来解决经验决策悔恨不可微的问题，但每次梯度计算都需调用计算昂贵的优化求解器，导致可扩展性极差。亟需一种既能保持决策质量又能彻底摆脱求解器依赖的训练方法。

Method: 提出基于测度变换原理的决策聚焦学习管线，构造全新的替代损失函数，使训练过程完全无需调用优化求解器。理论上证明了该方法具备Fisher一致性和过量风险界。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在决策质量上与当前最优方法相当，同时训练时间缩短数个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过测度变换原理构造的求解器无关替代损失，成功解决了predict-then-optimize框架中训练可扩展性的核心瓶颈，为大规模决策聚焦学习提供了实用方案。

Abstract: We propose a scalable method for training prediction (machine learning) models in the predict-then-optimize paradigm, where model outputs serve as coefficients for a subsequent linear optimization task. Directly minimizing the empirical decision regret is intractable for linear programming and combinatorial optimization since the decision mapping is piecewise constant, and the gradients are zero almost everywhere. While existing methods address this by smoothing the differentiation process, they suffer from scalability issues, since a computationally expensive solver call is required for every gradient evaluation. To address this, we propose a decision-focused learning pipeline based on a measure transformation principle, which yields a new surrogate loss that is completely optimization-solver-free during training. We establish theoretical guarantees, including Fisher consistency and excess risk bounds. Empirically, our method achieves decision quality competitive with state-of-the-art methods while reducing training time by orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [247] [Variational Consensus Monte Carlo for Bayesian Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19643)
*Julie Fendler,Francesca L. Crowe,Tom Marshall,Sylvia Richardson,Paul D. W. Kirk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套联邦学习环境下贝叶斯混合模型推断的完整流程，基于变分共识蒙特卡洛（CMC）方法，核心贡献包括：将变分CMC扩展到可推断聚类数的过拟合混合模型、设计跨节点簇匹配算法、提出多种聚合策略及其选择指南。实验表明，当本地数据结构反映全局聚类时，该方法恢复小簇的精度甚至优于合并数据的标准MCMC，并在英国老年人群电子健康记录中识别出多重疾病模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康数据因隐私、敏感性和共享限制无法集中汇总，传统的贝叶斯混合模型推断需要全量数据池化，因此需要一种在联邦学习框架下进行贝叶斯混合模型推断的有效方法。

Method: 采用共识蒙特卡洛（CMC）框架：各数据节点独立运行MCMC估计局部后验分布，再通过聚合步骤近似全局后验。具体创新包括：(1) 将Rabinovich等(2015)的变分CMC扩展至过拟合贝叶斯混合模型，无需共轭先验即可推断聚类数和全部参数；(2) 提出新型簇匹配算法，处理各本地数据集未必包含所有簇的跨节点场景；(3) 针对不同联邦约束设计了多种聚合推理策略；(4) 给出策略选择的实践指南。

Result: 综合模拟研究验证了框架有效性，并与最先进的联邦学习替代方案进行了比较。关键发现：当本地数据集的构成反映全局聚类结构时，该方法恢复小簇的精度优于对合并数据应用标准MCMC。方法在英国老年人群大规模电子健康记录中得到应用，成功识别出多重疾病共现模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的联邦贝叶斯混合模型框架在隐私受限场景下能有效推断聚类结构与参数，尤其擅长发现小规模聚类。该方法在真实世界大规模健康数据上的成功应用，证明了其在生物医学研究中的实用价值，为联邦环境下的无监督学习提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Motivated by the privacy, sensitivity and sharing limitations of health data, we present a comprehensive pipeline for inference of Bayesian mixture models within a federated learning setting, i.e. when data cannot be fully shared or pooled across compute nodes. We adopt a Consensus Monte Carlo (CMC) approach, in which an MCMC algorithm is run independently within each data silo to estimate local posterior distributions, which are then aggregated to approximate the posterior over the full data. The variational CMC approach of Rabinovich, Angelino and Jordan (2015) [1] frames the aggregation step as a variational inference problem, but their application to mixtures assumes the number of clusters and key mixture parameters to be known. Our main methodological contributions are: (i) an extension of variational CMC to over-fitted Bayesian mixture models that infer the number of clusters and all model parameters, without requiring conjugacy; (ii) novel cluster-matching algorithms suitable for cross-silo settings in which not every cluster appears in each local dataset; (iii) a number of inference strategies for the aggregation step, matched to different federated learning constraints; and (iv) guidelines for choosing among these in practice. A comprehensive simulation study validates the framework and allows us to compare to state-of-the-art federated learning alternatives. Notably, we show that when the composition of local datasets reflects the underlying clustering structure in the data, our approach can recover small clusters with greater accuracy than standard MCMC applied to the pooled data. We illustrate the framework on large-scale electronic health record data, identifying multi-morbidity patterns in a British geriatric population.

</details>


### [248] [AURA: Adaptive Uncertainty-aware Refinement for LLM-as-a-Judge Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19714)
*Zilong Zhang,Yi-Ting Hung,Weiyi He,Junxi Zhang,Lei Ding,Chi-Kuang Yeh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AURA 提出一种自适应不确定性感知的细化框架，通过迭代学习人类一致性信号并优先审查不确定比较对，在有限人工验证下审计 LLM 作为评判者的成对决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为评判者存在与人类偏好不一致的偏差，现有审计方法依赖事先存在的可靠标注子集或强监督信号，但在 LLM 评估场景中，初始分组可能继承评判偏差，且人工验证稀缺难以在规模上定义稳定分组。

Method: 提出 AURA 框架：将评判者的可信度建模为潜在变量，随着证据积累逐步细化；迭代地学习人类一致性信号、传播可靠证据，并根据不确定性优先选择需要人工审查的比较对。

Result: 在合成数据和真实成对 LLM 答案数据上进行了全面评估，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: AURA 能够在有限人工验证条件下有效审计和细化 LLM 评判决策，通过自适应不确定性感知机制提升评判结果与人类判断的一致性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges for open-ended generation, as large-scale human evaluation is often expensive and difficult to scale, yet their preferences remain imperfect proxies for human judgment. Existing auditing pipelines often assume that a reliable subset of examples or clean supervision signals are available beforehand, for example from human annotation, heuristic filtering, or the outputs of strong judges. In LLM evaluation, this assumption is fragile: the initial split may inherit judge bias, while human verification is typically too scarce to define stable groups at scale. We propose AURA, an adaptive uncertainty--aware refinement framework for auditing pairwise LLM--as--a--judge decisions under selected human verification. AURA iteratively learns a human-consistency signal, propagates reliable evidence, and prioritizes uncertain comparisons for human review. The key idea is to treat trust in a judge as a latent quantity that is progressively refined as evidence accumulates. We provide a compact formulation, a stable refinement procedure, and a comprehensive evaluation on both synthetic and real pairwise LLM-answer data.

</details>


### [249] [Stochastic Linear Contextual Bandits with Bounded Noise: A Set-Membership Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20022)
*Haonan Xu,Yingying Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对有界奖励噪声的随机线性上下文赌博机问题，提出了一种结合集合成员估计与乐观原则的SME-OFU算法，将遗憾界从亚高斯噪声下的$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$改进至$O(\log T)$，并通过仿真验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLCB研究通常假设亚高斯奖励噪声和有界期望奖励，得到$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$的最优遗憾界。然而在许多实际应用中，已实现/观测到的奖励本身是有界的，这意味着奖励噪声也是有界的。有界噪声比亚高斯条件更强、信息量更大，但尚未在SLCB文献中被明确利用。因此，作者旨在利用有界噪声这一更强条件，设计算法以获得更优的遗憾界。

Method: 提出SME-OFU算法：采用集合成员估计（Set-Membership Estimation, SME）进行不确定性量化，该方法利用噪声的有界性构建参数的一致置信集；在此基础上应用面对不确定性的乐观原则（Optimism in the Face of Uncertainty, OFU）进行决策。

Result: 理论分析表明，SME-OFU算法在有界奖励噪声条件下可获得$O(\log T)$的遗憾界，显著优于亚高斯噪声下$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$的最优界。这一改进并不矛盾，因为有界噪声是比亚高斯噪声更强的条件。仿真实验也证实，当奖励噪声有界时，SME-OFU相比为亚高斯噪声设计的基准算法具有经验性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过明确利用有界奖励噪声这一更强条件，SME-OFU算法实现了遗憾界从多项式到对数的显著改进，揭示了噪声结构信息在在线学习中的关键价值。

Abstract: This paper considers stochastic linear contextual bandits (SLCB) with bounded reward noise. Existing works typically assume sub-Gaussian reward noise and bounded expected rewards, under which the optimal regret bound scales as $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ in terms of horizon $T$. However, in many applications, realized/observed rewards are also naturally bounded, implying bounded reward noise. Bounded noise is more informative than the sub-Gaussian condition but has not been leveraged explicitly in the SLCB literature. In this paper, we propose a novel algorithm SME-OFU by utilizing an uncertainty quantification method called set-membership estimation (SME) and applying the principle of optimism in the face of uncertainty (OFU). Our algorithm enjoys an improved regret bound $O(\log T)$. Notice that this does not contradict the existing optimal bound $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ for sub-Gaussian noise because bounded noise is a stronger condition. Finally, simulations show empirical improvements of SME-OFU over a benchmark algorithm designed for sub-Gaussian noise when the reward noise is bounded.

</details>


### [250] [AK-MCS-C2 : Active Kriging Monte Carlo Simulation method with conformal certification for failure probability estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20191)
*Edgar Jaber,Vincent Chabridon,Mathilde Mougeot*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合主动Kriging蒙特卡罗模拟（AK-MCS）与共形预测（conformal prediction）的新型主动学习框架，用于结构可靠性分析中的失效概率估计。该框架采用自适应交叉共形策略和J+GP共形估计器，能提供无分布假设的预测误差保证，在小样本和稀有事件场景下显著提升失效概率估计的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准AK-MCS方法在极限状态面附近对样本分类时缺乏严格的误差保证，尤其在稀有事件（小失效概率）场景下，代理模型的不确定性量化不足会导致失效概率估计不可靠。本文旨在通过引入共形预测机制，提供一种具有分布无关误差保证的解决方案，以提高稀有事件失效概率估计的准确性和稳健性。

Method: 提出一种自适应交叉共形（adaptive cross-conformal）主动学习框架，将AK-MCS与共形预测融合。核心包括：(1) 针对小样本场景和Kriging代理模型专门设计的交叉共形策略；(2) 采用J+GP共形估计器（conformal estimator）对极限状态面附近的样本进行分类；(3) 利用共形预测框架提供无分布假设（distribution-free）的预测误差保证，从而增强不确定性量化能力。

Result: 在多个公认基准测试上的可复现数值实验表明，所提方法相较于经典AK-MCS方法，在失效概率估计精度和鲁棒性上均有提升，尤其在稀有事件场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: 将共形预测与AK-MCS结合是提升结构可靠性分析中失效概率估计质量的有效途径。该框架的无分布误差保证特性使其在稀有事件、小样本等实际工程场景中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce a novel active-learning framework for failure probability estimation in structural reliability analysis that integrates Active Kriging Monte Carlo simulation with conformal prediction. The proposed approach employs an adaptive cross-conformal strategy specifically designed for small-sample settings and kriging surrogate models using the J+GP conformal estimator. Unlike standard AK-MCS methods, the proposed framework provides distribution-free guarantees on prediction errors, leading to more reliable classification of samples near the limit-state surface. This improved uncertainty quantification enhances both the accuracy and robustness of failure probability estimates, especially for rare-event regimes where such efficiency is crucial. Reproducible numerical results illustrate the effectiveness of the method and also compare it to classical approaches on well-established benchmarks.

</details>


### [251] [Off-Policy Evaluation for Missingness-Aware Policies in MDPs with Rewards Missing Not at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20206)
*Ziheng Wei,Annie Qu,Rui Miao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文研究离线强化学习中奖励值存在非随机缺失（MNAR）情况下的离线策略评估（OPE）问题，提出利用未来状态作为影子变量和桥函数来识别并恢复缺失奖励的条件均值，并基于Fitted-Q-Evaluation构建了一致性估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗、营销等实际场景中，记录的批次数据往往因记录稀疏、审查机制等原因导致即时奖励缺失，且缺失机制非随机（MNAR），即使条件于状态和动作仍存在选择偏差，传统的基于可忽略性假设的方法失效。

Method: 首先形式化一个奖励依赖的倾向性模型，利用未来状态作为影子变量识别全数据条件均值奖励；进而引入桥函数，在不显式建模MNAR机制的情况下恢复条件均值奖励，并通过min-max过程估计以避免双重采样；最终提出FQE风格的OPE估计器，允许目标策略依赖历史的缺失指示变量。

Result: 证明了所提OPE估计器的一致性并给出有限样本误差界；在模拟数据和MIMIC-III脓毒症真实数据上的实验表明，该方法相比现有方法具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 通过影子变量和桥函数可以有效解决MNAR奖励下的离线策略评估问题，恢复的条件均值奖励可无缝嵌入FQE框架，方法在理论和实证上均表现出色。

Abstract: In offline Reinforcement Learning, immediate rewards in logged batch data are often unobserved due to sparse or irregular record-keeping, or censored beyond certain reward values. This issue arises in practical settings, including health care and marketing. We investigate off-policy evaluation (OPE) in finite-horizon Markov decision processes when rewards are missing not at random (MNAR), which breaks ignorability and induces selection bias even after conditioning on states and actions. To address this, we formalize a reward-dependent propensity model and use future states as shadow variables to identify the full-data conditional mean reward. We further introduce a bridge function that recovers the conditional mean reward without explicitly modeling the MNAR mechanism, and estimate it via a min-max procedure to avoid double sampling. Building upon these identification results, we propose an Fitted-Q-Evaluation-style estimator that propagates the recovered rewards while allowing target policies to depend on past missingness indicators. Finally, we establish consistency and finite-sample error bounds for our OPE estimator, and show through experiments the strong performance of our method compared to existing methods on simulated and MIMIC-III Sepsis data.

</details>


### [252] [Statistical Properties of Training & Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20299)
*Itay Lavie,Noam Levi,Yonatan Kahn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从物理学视角审视深度学习的关键特征与意外现象，重点回顾了神经标度律，并讨论其与物理学问题中约束条件和归纳偏置之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习虽然在众多实际任务中取得了前所未有的性能，但其成功往往违背经典统计学的直觉。作者旨在从物理学视角系统性地解释深度学习的核心特征与"意外"，帮助理解构建深度学习模型时所涉及的诸多设计选择及其合理性。

Method: 采用物理学启发的分析视角，对深度学习模型的构建选择进行梳理与论证，并重点综述神经标度律现象，探讨其与物理问题中固有的约束条件及归纳偏置之间的交互关系。

Result: 揭示深度学习模型中的若干关键特征与反直觉现象可以通过物理学视角获得理解；神经标度律与物理问题中的约束和归纳偏置之间存在复杂的相互作用。

Conclusion: 物理学视角可为深度学习的设计选择提供理论支撑和合理性论证，神经标度律与物理先验的融合是机器学习应用于物理问题时的重要考量方向。

Abstract: Deep learning has managed to evade numerous intuitions from classical statistics to achieve unprecedented performance on a number of real-world tasks. In this article, we investigate the key features and surprises of deep learning from a physics-informed perspective, taking care to point out and justify where possible the many choices inherent in constructing a deep learning model. In particular, we review the phenomenon of neural scaling laws and discuss their interplay with the constraints and inductive biases which may be present when applying machine learning to problems in physics.

</details>


### [253] [SSH-Net: A Deep Neural Network for Predicting Failure Time Distribution Functions under Competing Risks with Application to GPU Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20451)
*Jie Min,Yueyao Wang,Mengkun Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化分段风险深度神经网络（SSH-Net），针对竞争风险场景下多层级复杂系统的故障时间预测问题，通过将神经网络结构与数据层次结构相关联，利用独立子网络处理不同协变量组，并基于特定原因竞争风险模型输出风险函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争风险数据在工程领域普遍存在，深度神经网络虽灵活但超参数调优困难；此外，当工程系统具有多层级物理结构时，将所有层级简单合并为单一输入组会丢失关键信息。因此需要一种能够匹配数据结构层次、降低调参难度的竞争风险预测方法。

Method: 提出SSH-Net模型，核心思路是将神经网络结构嵌入数据的分组结构——不同协变量组通过独立的子网络（segmented sub-networks）分别处理，再整合输出特定原因的风险函数。模型基于特定原因竞争风险框架构建，以带惩罚项的对数似然函数作为损失函数进行训练。

Result: 通过模拟研究，以Brier评分、AUC和预测的特定原因累积发生函数的RMSE评估SSH-Net的预测精度，结果验证了模型的有效性。此外，利用Titan GPU故障时间数据进一步展示了模型预测故障时间分布函数的能力。

Conclusion: SSH-Net通过结构化分段设计有效应对多层级复杂系统的竞争风险建模问题，在模拟和真实GPU故障数据上均表现出良好的预测性能，为工程领域的故障时间预测提供了一种有效的深度学习解决方案。

Abstract: Competing risks are commonly observed in engineering fields and can bring challenges to time-to-event data modeling when the application scenarios are complicated. Recently, deep neural networks have received great attention for prediction with competing risks, due to their flexibility and high learning capability. However, the complexity of neural network structure brings extra difficulty in hyperparameter tuning based on different data inputs. Additionally, when an engineered system has complex physical structures with multiple hierarchical levels, treating all structural levels as a single group of inputs may fail to capture critical information. To address the issues, we propose a Structured Segmented Hazard Deep Neural Network (SSH-Net) for failure time prediction under cause-specific competing risks framework. Our approach associates neural network structure with data structures, and allows different covariate groups to impact the failure prediction through separate sub-networks. The neural network is constructed based on a cause-specific competing risks model. The SSH-Net outputs cause-specific hazard functions, and utilizes the penalized log-likelihood as the loss function. The prediction accuracy of SSH-Net is validated through simulation studies by evaluating the Brier score, the area under receiver operating characteristic curves (AUC), and the root mean square error (RMSE) of the predicted cause-specific cumulative incident function. We further demonstrate the model's ability to predict failure time distribution functions using the Titan GPU failure time data.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“计算可识别性”（computational identifiability）框架，将传统渐近理论的可识别性重新定义为有限计算搜索过程：若在给定先验分布和搜索过程下，能在期望误差容限内经验地找到估计量，则可识别性即被满足，适用于小样本、模糊图标准、混合观测-干预数据及反事实估计等实际场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果可识别性（theoretical identifiability）依赖渐近性质、无限数据等理想化条件，无法回答有限样本、模糊图结构、混合数据类型等实际场景中的细粒度识别问题。作者旨在弥合理论可识别性与实际计算可行性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出“计算可识别性”框架：将可识别性定义为一个有限计算搜索过程，该过程在参数先验分布和搜索程序的双重条件下，尝试在期望误差容限内经验地找到目标估计量。若搜索成功，则满足可识别性。通过多个实验验证该框架在小样本、模糊图准则、混合观测-干预数据及反事实估计等场景下的适用性。代码开源在GitHub。

Result: 实验表明，计算可识别性框架能够在传统理论可识别性无法给出明确答案的多种实际场景中（小样本、模糊图标准、混合数据、反事实估计）回答精细化的识别问题，提供了更具操作性的可识别性判定。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性为因果推断提供了一种更贴近实际计算约束的替代性可识别性定义，将可识别性从纯粹的理论性质转变为条件化的经验搜索问题，对有限样本和复杂数据场景下的因果效应识别具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [255] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GUARD提出了一种门控不确定性感知路由蒸馏框架，通过实例级上下文路由器动态选择教师模型，并结合不确定性门控温度机制自动调节蒸馏强度，从而将分布失配的基础模型知识有效迁移至轻量级预测器，在四个气候关键领域实现高精度边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFM）虽蕴含丰富的通用时序动态知识，但存在两大瓶颈：(1) 零样本应用于特定科学领域时分布严重失配；(2) 计算成本过高，无法部署于边缘传感器网络。核心问题是：如何从失配的基础模型中提取潜在结构知识，以训练轻量级的专用预测器。

Method: GUARD框架将多教师蒸馏重新构建为实例级决策过程，包含两个自适应机制：(1) 上下文路由器（Contextual Router），基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关的教师模型，利用不同基础模型间的互补性；(2) 不确定性门控温度机制（Uncertainty-Gated Temperature），作为"断路器"，当教师置信度与领域真实情况偏离时自动衰减蒸馏强度。

Result: 在气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源电网四个气候关键领域评估，GUARD相较于固定权重的多教师蒸馏基线显著降低RMSE。即使教师模型因分布偏移而零样本精度欠佳，仍能成功蒸馏知识。这些领域失配的教师模型在28.5%的最难实例上表现优于全局最优的基础模型，起到了关键纠偏作用。

Conclusion: GUARD证明了领域失配的基础模型仍可作为有效的纠偏信号源，通过实例级自适应蒸馏，可以训练出适合资源受限边缘部署的高精度轻量级科学预测模型。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [256] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPSD是一种边缘端提示词蒸馏流水线，利用4-bit量化的小语言模型在设备端压缩用户提示，减少云端LLM的输入token数，在保证回复质量非劣效的前提下实现每调用节省70-270微瓦时的净能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的prefill阶段消耗大量云端能源，而许多消费级对话提示中包含礼貌标记、道歉性开场、重复和建立融洽关系的语言——这些对人际交流重要但对机器推理边际信息量低，作者将这一现象称为"社会-语义鸿沟"（Social-Semantic Gap）。现有方案未在边缘端系统性地解决这一冗余问题。

Method: 提出SPSD（Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation）流水线：在边缘设备上部署4-bit量化的小语言模型（SLM，具体为Gemma-2-2B-Instruct Q4_K_M），对用户原始提示进行语义蒸馏压缩，保留核心语义和情感基调，再传输至云端LLM（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）进行推理。对安全敏感领域通过基于规则的gate进行保守路由（passthrough），避免关键信息丢失。

Result: 在248条提示语料上评测：①每个蒸馏调用平均节省99.9个输入token，全部146次蒸馏调用均产生正向节省；②盲法LLM-as-judge评分（15分量表）显示蒸馏路径非劣效于原始路径（预设1分非劣效边界），43%平局、28%蒸馏胜出、29%原始胜出；③余弦相似度表现混合：均值0.682，中位数0.712，54.1%的配对高于0.70参考阈值。

Conclusion: SPSD证明，在设备端进行提示蒸馏可以在保持回复质量（满足非劣效性边界）的前提下显著降低云端LLM的输入token成本，为云边协同推理提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [257] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对医学扩散模型Med-DDPM在三代NVIDIA GPU上进行了全面的性能分析，揭示了训练瓶颈在于cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM核的低效内存访问及Tensor Core利用率不足，并通过TF32 Tensor Core激活与3D channels-last布局两项优化，在A100上实现最高100倍的SM周期缩减，且不影响合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D MRI合成中表现优异，但其部署因数百次U-Net评估和高度异构的核行为而消耗大量GPU资源。现有工作缺乏对医学扩散模型在GPU架构层面的系统性能分析，导致优化缺乏针对性指导。

Method: 以Med-DDPM为基准，在NVIDIA V100、A100、H100三代架构上进行内核级运行时分解析，分析指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、warp级活动及profiler优先级评分；基于分析结果，评估两项架构感知优化：TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last内存布局。

Result: 训练过程被cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM核主导；优化后SM周期缩减最高100倍，动态指令减少100倍，Tensor Core利用率从1.45倍提升至9.98倍，IPC提升7%（A100），且合成质量无退化。

Conclusion: 通过对医学扩散模型的底层性能瓶颈进行精细诊断，架构感知的数值精度与内存布局优化可大幅提升训练效率，为在资源受限环境下部署高质量3D MRI合成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [258] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 信息格学习（ILL）通过交替投影与提升学习概率信号的可解释规则。本文揭示了ILL学习的概率规则可自然地解释为概率图模型：分区诱导确定性商变量，规则为该商变量的边际律，规则集构成可解释抽象上的边际约束集合。一般提升是满足约束的所有联合分布族，特殊提升则选择最大无知重构（L2均匀性原则，与最大熵密切相关）。在Shannon熵提升下，约束产生对数线性因子图，其因子由学习到的抽象索引。信息格本身并非贝叶斯网络，其边编码的是抽象的细化与粗化关系，而非条件依赖。因此，ILL最适合理解为商变量上基于约束的可解释因子图的结构学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ILL原本作为一种学习可解释规则的方法被提出，但其与概率图模型（PGM）之间的深层联系尚未被充分阐明。本文旨在揭示ILL所学的概率规则如何自然地映射到因子图框架中，从而为ILL提供严格的PGM理论解释，并澄清信息格与贝叶斯网络之间的本质区别。

Method: 作者将ILL的核心组件映射到PGM概念：将分区映射为确定性商变量，规则映射为商变量的边际分布，规则集映射为一组边际约束。在此基础上分析了一般提升（所有满足约束的联合分布族）和特殊提升（基于L2均匀性原则的最大无知重构），并将其与最大熵原理关联。进一步，在Shannon熵提升下推导出对数线性因子图表示，并论证信息格的边结构编码的是抽象层次间的细化/粗化关系而非条件依赖，从而区别于贝叶斯网络。

Result: ILL可被严格解释为商变量上基于约束的可解释因子图的结构学习框架。一般提升定义了一个由边际约束刻画的可行分布族，特殊提升的L2均匀性原则与最大熵原理紧密对应。在Shannon熵下，约束集产生一个对数线性因子图，因子节点由学习到的抽象（商变量）索引。信息格的图结构并非概率依赖图，而是编码抽象粒度的偏序关系。

Conclusion: ILL应被视为一种面向可解释约束型因子图的结构学习方法，其与最大熵模型和因子图具有内在一致性。这一视角不仅澄清了ILL与图模型的关系，还为推理、可辨识性以及符号-概率混合学习等方向开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [259] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于双参数Weibull框架分析Transformer权重分布，本文研究了AdamW训练中Weibull尺度参数λ的增长、过冲与松弛现象。作者推导了权重范数平方的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力），发现对齐力主导上升阶段，与衰减力趋于平衡时引发松弛。此外，提出样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力，并观察到λ峰值与训练数据一致性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weibull尺度参数λ在AdamW训练中呈现增长-过冲-松弛的非单调现象，其动力学机制尚不清晰。理解这一行为有助于揭示自适应优化器下的权重演化规律，并为权重分布诊断提供理论支撑。

Method: 首先，从AdamW更新规则出发，将权重范数平方的变化分解为三个一阶力项：对齐力（权重与自适应更新方向的相关性）、注入力（自适应步长幅值贡献）和衰减力（去耦权重衰减贡献）。在自训练的Pythia-70M模型上，利用真实优化器矩统计各力项占比。其次，将范数平方与Weibull尺度参数λ的映射误差分解为桥接分量和积分分量。最后，针对实际模型无法获取优化器矩的问题，提出样条位移方法，从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 在Pythia-70M的四次随机种子实验中，对齐力主导λ上升阶段，贡献绝对力预算的88-94%，且对超级权重移除鲁棒。在饱和阶段，对齐力与衰减力趋于平衡，解释了增长向松弛的过渡。RMS到Weibull的重构偏移可分解为桥接与积分分量，在密集采样区域合计约5-6%。样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力的准确率达92-94%，约为朴素两点基线的两倍。此外，初步实验表明λ峰值随训练数据一致性变化。

Conclusion: AdamW训练中Weibull尺度参数λ的动力学由对齐力、注入力和衰减力三者共同决定，其中对齐力主导增长、对齐-衰减平衡触发松弛。提出的分析方法可拓展至无法获取优化器矩的实际模型，为权重分布演化提供了可解释的力学框架，并揭示了数据依赖性对权重尺度增长的影响。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [260] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多元零膨胀高斯（ZIG）分布作为分布估计算法（EDA）的采样分布，首次将EDA推广到稀疏参数空间，实现了稀疏模式与活跃值的联合优化，无需手工设计的稀疏算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDA在连续参数空间已很成熟，但从未被推广到稀疏空间。现有稀疏黑盒优化器重新引入了手工设计的稀疏算子、双层方案、置零阈值等，这恰恰违背了EDA避免手工算子设计的初衷。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出以多元零膨胀高斯（ZIG）分布作为EDA的采样分布。ZIG模型包含隐高斯变量，具有独立的指示维度（控制稀疏性）和数值维度（控制活跃值），可同时表示稀疏模式、活跃参数间的相关性以及两者之间的交互，实现无层级的联合优化。作者证明了该模型隐参数的可识别性（区别于缺失数据场景），并设计了基于摊销反演的实用估计器。

Result: 估计器能准确恢复隐相关结构。在Lunar Lander基准测试上，ZIG-EDA比稠密高斯EDA、手工设计的稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA收敛更快、最终回报更高，且仅需少量活跃参数。

Conclusion: ZIG-EDA成功将EDA范式扩展到稀疏优化领域，避免了手工稀疏算子设计，实现了稀疏结构与参数值的联合进化，为稀疏黑盒优化提供了原则性的概率建模方案。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [261] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Spiced Self-Play方法，将少量人类驾驶示范（仅30分钟）作为自博弈强化学习的正则化项，在最小安全目标达成奖励基础上，以仅需模仿学习2500分之一的数据量，训练出与人类驾驶惯例兼容的自动驾驶策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自博弈强化学习虽能无需人类数据训练驾驶策略，但学到的驾驶行为可能形成与人类不兼容的"异类"驾驶惯例。现有解决方案依赖大量奖励工程和领域随机化，脆弱且劳动密集。本文旨在以极少的人类示范数据解决行为对齐问题。

Method: 将人类驾驶示范作为正则化目标叠加在极简的安全目标达成奖励之上（Spiced Self-Play）。自博弈提供大规模廉价仿真训练，少量人类数据（30分钟）作为"调味料"引导策略行为与人类惯例对齐。策略在单张消费级GPU上训练仅需15小时。

Result: 所得策略能与留出的人类轨迹协调配合，仅使用30分钟人类示范（比同类模仿学习方法少2500倍），在单张消费级GPU上15小时完成训练。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据在自博弈框架中如同"好汤中的调味料"，一点就大有裨益。该方法在数据效率和训练成本上具有显著优势，为自动驾驶策略训练提供了一种实用且高效的范式。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [262] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMUSE 是一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，通过先使用低成本临床数据做证据分类并量化不确定性，仅当不确定性超过阈值时才依次引入 MRI/PET 特征，利用 Dempster-Shafer 理论融合模态信念与不确定性，从而在保持诊断精度的同时减少 50-90% 的昂贵影像使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD 早期诊断依赖多模态数据（临床评估、MRI、PET），但 MRI 和 PET 获取成本高且并非普遍可及，在真实临床流程中实现全模态推断不切实际。因此需要一种能自适应决定何时需要额外模态、在保证准确率的前提下降低数据采集成本的方法。

Method: ProMUSE 分阶段工作：(1) 先用低成本临床数据做证据分类，通过 Dirichlet 主观逻辑模型量化不确定性；(2) 当不确定性超过学习到的阈值时，逐步引入 MRI 或 PET 特征；(3) 利用 Dempster-Shafer 理论融合各模态的信念与不确定性，得到校准后的多模态预测。这一分阶段采集策略实现了自适应模态选择。

Result: 在 ADNI、AIBL 和 OASIS 数据集上的 CN-AD、CN-MCI、MCI-AD 任务中，ProMUSE 达到与全模态基线相当或更优的准确率，同时将 MRI/PET 使用量减少 50-90%，带来显著的成本节约。

Conclusion: ProMUSE 是一种实用、不确定性感知且资源高效的 AD 筛查方案，为真实临床环境中的早期诊断提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

</details>


### [263] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cAPM，一种结合主动学习与持续学习的AI辅助起搏标测方法，通过任务无关的替代神经网络在不同VT目标间迁移知识，大幅减少所需起搏位点数并提高定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室速导管消融中的起搏标测需要临床医生反复起搏并解读心电图，效率低下。现有主动学习AI方法虽能减少起搏位点，但每次新目标都需从头训练，无法在同一患者多个VT或跨患者间迁移知识。

Method: 提出cAPM方法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 任务无关的替代神经网络，学习从起搏位点到12导联心电图形态的映射；(2) 主动学习策略，为每个目标选择最具信息量的起搏位点来精化替代模型；(3) 持续学习策略，在顺序处理多个目标时保留先前知识。评估中分别测试了有/无过往数据回放两种变体。

Result: 在包含不同生理条件和心室几何形态的仿真测试平台上，cAPM使用平均4.5个起搏位点即可达到81%的临床容差内（5 mm精度）定位概率；相比之下，现有最优主动学习方法需要13.7个起搏位点，仅达38%概率。

Conclusion: cAPM通过持续学习和知识迁移显著提升了起搏标测的效率与精度，为向临床前和临床研究转化提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Ventricular tachycardia is a life-threatening rhythm disorder and a major cause of sudden cardiac death. Pace-mapping is a clinical procedure for identifying the intervention target during catheter ablation of VT. It requires clinicians to pace different sites in the ventricles and rapidly interpret the resulting electrocardiograms to determine where to pace next or whether a target site has been identified. Active learning AI models have been proposed to guide clinicians to the next pacing site, showing promise in reducing the number of pacing sites and improving the efficiency of pace-mapping. Existing methods require retraining each target without the ability to transfer knowledge across multiple VTs within the same patient or across patients. We introduce cAPM for continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping to capture and transfer knowledge accumulated from past pace-mapping data to reduce the number of pace-mapping data needed for future target VTs. This is made possible by a task-agnostic surrogate neural network that learns the mapping from pacing sites to 12-lead ECG morphology, an active-learning strategy that refines this surrogate model by selecting the most informative pacing site for each target, and a continual learning strategy to do so sequentially while retaining knowledge from prior targets. Evaluated on an in-silico testbed consisting of sequentially-presented localization tasks across different physiological conditions and ventricular geometries, cAPM with and without replay of past data samples achieved an 81% probability of localizing within clinical tolerance (5 mm accuracy) using 4.5 pace-mapping sites, compared to the state-of-the-art active-learning method achieving 38% probability using 13.7 pacing sites. These results provide a strong basis for preparing cAPM towards in-vivo preclinical and clinical studies where it can be used to guide pace-mapping.

</details>


### [264] [Algebraic Dead Directions in LayerNorm Transformers: A Forward-Pass-Only Diagnostic at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19491)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar,P. J. Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LayerNorm Transformer中，LayerNorm仿射参数的逆缩放方向是后归一化中心化激活协方差的精确代数核，构成参数空间中的"死方向"（Fisher信息矩阵退化方向）。该方向可直接从LayerNorm的缩放参数读出，无需前向/反向传播或特征分解，是最廉价的死方向检测方法。在14个预训练Transformer上验证了该发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer通常位于损失景观的奇异极小值附近，Fisher信息矩阵沿某些"死方向"退化。传统定位死方向需要前向传播和激活特征分解或基于采样的复杂度估计，计算成本高，且无法仅从网络参数直接获得方向。本文旨在寻找一种仅从参数即可读取的死方向，特别是针对LayerNorm Transformer。

Method: 作者从数学上证明，LayerNorm仿射变换的逆缩放方向 γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖ 是最终层归一化之后中心化激活协方差的精确代数核（对任意输入分布成立），从而在参数空间中诱导出一个死方向。该方向仅需读取LN的缩放参数即可获得，无需任何前向/反向传播或特征分解。在9个LayerNorm和5个RMSNorm模型（160M-35B参数，语言和视觉任务）上进行了验证：随机初始化时比对预测方向与SVD测量的底部奇异方向；训练后检测沿该方向的协方差特征值加深情况以及额外死方向的出现。

Result: 随机初始化时，9/9个LayerNorm模型上预测方向与实际底部奇异方向匹配至小数点后4位；5/5个RMSNorm模型（缺乏均值减法投影器）正确未出现该方向。训练后，沿该方向的协方差特征值加深约10³倍，并出现更多死方向。两个封闭形式的推论：(1) 残差流的最小奇异值在13/14个Transformer的逐块传递中保持，唯一例外(Gemma 4-31B)被同一读出方法精确定位为真正的死方向；(2) 该核方向的存在与否可直接从参数判定Transformer使用的归一化类型。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一种仅从LayerNorm缩放参数读取死方向的廉价方法，揭示了LayerNorm架构中固有的代数结构——逆缩放方向作为激活协方差的精确核，且该性质从初始化到训练后持续存在并加深。该方法不仅提供了理解Transformer损失景观几何的新工具，还可用于判定归一化类型和检测死方向，具有实际工程价值。

Abstract: Pretrained transformers sit near singular minima of the loss, where the Fisher information metric degenerates along dead directions: directions in parameter space along which the directional Fisher vanishes. Locating such a direction normally needs a forward pass and an eigendecomposition of activations, or a sampling-based complexity estimate; none returns a direction computable from the network's parameters alone. We give one, for LayerNorm transformers. The inverse-scale direction $γ^{-1}/\|γ^{-1}\|$ of the LayerNorm affine is an exact algebraic kernel of the post-final-norm centred activation covariance, for any input distribution, and induces a corresponding dead direction in parameter space. It is read from the LN scale parameter alone, with no forward or backward pass and no eigensolve: the cheapest dead-direction read, specific to LayerNorm. We test it on $14$ pretrained transformers ($9$ LayerNorm, $5$ RMSNorm; $160$M-$35$B; language and vision objectives). At random initialisation the predicted direction matches the measured bottom singular direction (one forward pass, direct SVD) to four decimal places on $9/9$ LayerNorm models, and is correctly absent on $5/5$ RMSNorm models, which lack the mean-subtraction projector that creates it. On the trained checkpoint the covariance eigenvalue along this direction deepens by ${\sim}10^3\times$ and further dead directions open; the random-init-to-trained gap is a one-forward-pass, per-checkpoint readout of singular structure along the predicted coordinate. Two consequences follow in closed form: the residual stream's smallest singular value is preserved block-to-block on $13/14$ transformers measured on their own input distribution, the one exception (Gemma$4$-$31$B) a genuine dead direction the same read pinpoints; and the kernel direction's presence classifies a transformer's normalisation from the parameters alone.

</details>


### [265] [Protein Representation Learning with Secondary-Structure and Energy-Filtered Hydrogen-Bond Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19374)
*Mohamed Mouhajir,Limei Wang,El Houcine Bergou,Hajar El Hammouti,Lamiae Azizi,Dongqi Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SSProNet，一种基于二级结构感知的图神经网络，通过融入二级结构注释和基于能量筛选的氢键边构建，来学习蛋白质表征，在多个基准上优于现有图方法，并具备更好的生物可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质建模的图方法大多仅依赖序列邻接或几何邻近性，未能充分反映蛋白质折叠中二级结构（α-螺旋、β-折叠）以及氢键相互作用所编码的局部 motif 和长程耦合等核心原理。

Method: 提出SSProNet：在残基级节点表征中增强二级结构分配信息；图边根据氢键相互作用的能量强度进行筛选构建；使模型能够同时捕获局部结构上下文和远程耦合。

Result: 在常用蛋白质基准数据集上，SSProNet相比现有图方法取得了持续一致的性能提升，且学习到的图连通性与已知结构motif对齐，增强了生物学可解释性。

Conclusion: 将二级结构信息与能量筛选的氢键拓扑相结合，为蛋白质表征学习提供了一种有效的归纳偏置，建议未来在此方向进一步探索。

Abstract: Graph-based representations are widely used in protein modeling, yet many existing approaches rely primarily on sequence adjacency or geometric proximity, which only partially reflect the principles governing protein folding. Proteins instead adopt complex three-dimensional conformations organized around secondary structure elements, such as $α$-helices and $β$-sheets, which encode recurring local motifs and stabilizing hydrogen-bond interactions. In this work, we introduce a secondary-structure-aware graph neural network for protein representation learning. Residue-level node representations are augmented with secondary structure assignments, and graph edges are constructed from hydrogen-bond interactions filtered by their energetic strength. This design enables the model to capture both local structural context and long-range couplings that are central to protein stability and function. We evaluate the proposed approach on commonly used protein benchmarks and observe consistent improvements over existing graph-based methods. In addition, the resulting graph representations offer enhanced biological interpretability, as the learned connectivity aligns with established structural motifs. These findings suggest that incorporating secondary structure and energy-filtered hydrogen-bond topology provides an effective inductive bias for protein representation learning. The code is released at https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet

</details>


### [266] [Physics-Informed Discovery of Yield Functions in Plasticity via Convex Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19375)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Donghyuk Cho,Jecheon Yu,Jeong Whan Yoon,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息驱动的框架，从全场位移和反力数据中直接发现各向异性屈服函数，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量或预设参数形式。该方法将屈服函数作为弹塑性应力积分中的凸神经网络组件，通过可微应力更新和多工况力平衡损失进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性屈服函数的识别面临三大挑战：屈服行为无法在全场力学测量中直接观测；方向性标定需要大量不同加载方向；选择恰当的解析函数形式本身就很困难。现有方法通常依赖应力空间直接监督或预设参数化形式，限制了其灵活性和适用范围。

Method: 提出一种物理信息框架，核心思路是将屈服函数作为弹塑性应力积分内部的力学约束组件进行识别，而非通过应力空间直接监督。具体而言：1）采用凸神经网络表示屈服函数，强制满足凸性、一阶正齐次性以及拉压对称性；2）通过可微应力更新算法，将屈服函数嵌入弹塑性本构积分中；3）利用多加载工况下的全场位移数据和反力数据，以物理信息力平衡损失函数进行训练，无需应力或塑性应变标签。

Result: 通过有限元基准测试验证了框架的有效性，涵盖 von Mises、Hill 1948 和 Yld2000-2d 三种屈服函数。评估维度包括：屈服轨迹一致性、位移噪声敏感性、塑性激活应力状态的可辨识性、认知不确定性以及多项式代理模型部署。结果表明该框架能够从位移和力数据中准确恢复各向异性屈服面。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一条力学约束驱动的路径，能够在保持识别组件处于弹塑性应力积分结构内的前提下，从位移和力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，为无需应力标签的本构模型识别开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Identifying anisotropic yield functions remains challenging since yielding is not directly observed in full-field mechanical measurements, directional calibration can require many loading directions, and selecting an appropriate analytical form is nontrivial. This study proposes a physics-informed framework for discovering yield functions from full-field displacement data and reaction force data, without stress observations, plastic strain measurements, direct yield surface data, or a prescribed parametric yield function. The framework identifies the yield function as a mechanically constrained constitutive component inside elastoplastic stress integration, rather than through direct stress-space supervision. The yield function is represented by a convex neural network that enforces convexity and positive homogeneity of degree one while imposing the assumed tension-compression symmetry, and this neural yield function is trained with a differentiable stress update and a physics-informed force equilibrium loss across multiple loading cases. The proposed framework is validated using finite element (FE) benchmark studies with von Mises, Hill 1948, and Yld2000-2d yield functions, assessing yield contour agreement, displacement-noise sensitivity, identifiability through plastically active stress states, epistemic uncertainty, and polynomial-surrogate deployment. This study provides a mechanics-constrained pathway for discovering anisotropic yield functions from displacement and force data while keeping the identified component within the structure of elastoplastic stress integration.

</details>


### [267] [Cost-Optimal LLM Routing with Limited User Feedback under User Satisfaction Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19376)
*Herbert Woisetschläger,Arastun Mammadli,Ryan Zhang,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLARouter 是一种在线路由算法，从生产系统中稀疏、单边的用户反馈中学习成本最优策略，在提供严格 SLA 合规保证的同时，相比现有基线降低高达 2.2 倍的运营成本，且无需针对每个基准进行调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理成本快速增长，而用户期望高质量回复（通常由 SLA 正式约束），成本与质量之间存在根本性矛盾。现有成本感知的 LLM 路由方法依赖完整反馈信号、离线训练、大量针对特定工作负载的调优，且大多缺乏 SLA 保证或推理时自适应能力。

Method: 提出 SLARouter，一种在线路由算法，能够从生产环境中稀疏、单边的用户反馈中在线学习成本最优的路由策略。该算法在理论上同时保证成本最优性和严格 SLA 合规，并具备推理时自适应能力，无需离线训练或针对特定工作负载的调优。

Result: 在广泛的 LLM 基准测试中，SLARouter 无需针对每个基准进行调优即可满足 SLA 约束，运营成本相比现有基线降低高达 2.2 倍。

Conclusion: SLARouter 成功解决了 LLM 服务中成本与质量之间的核心矛盾，通过稀疏反馈在线学习提供理论保证的 SLA 合规路由，显著降低了运营成本，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Inference costs for large language model (LLM) applications are rapidly growing, driven by surging demand and rising infrastructure cost. Users expect high-quality responses, and in commercial settings this is formally codified in Service Level Agreements (SLAs), creating a fundamental tension between cost and quality. Recent progress on cost-aware LLM request routing has shown potential to resolve this tension, but existing approaches rely on complete feedback signals, offline training, extensive per-workload tuning, and most lack SLA guarantees or inference-time adaptivity. We introduce SLARouter, an online routing algorithm that learns a cost-optimal policy from the sparse, one-sided user feedback available in production systems. SLARouter provides theoretical guarantees for both cost optimality and strict SLA compliance. Experiments across a wide range of LLM benchmarks show that SLARouter satisfies SLA constraints without the need for per-benchmark tuning, reducing operating cost by up to 2.2x over existing baselines.

</details>


### [268] [Emyx: Fast and efficient all-atom protein generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19377)
*Nicholas J. Williams,Ward Haddadin,Matteo P. Ferla,Constantin Schneider,Nicholas B. Woodall,Ruby Sedgwick,Christian D. Madsen,Andrew L. Hopkins,Edward O. Pyzer-Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Emyx是一种仅140M参数的条件流匹配模型，用于计算酶设计。它用轻量条件表示和稀疏连接替代传统预测模型的重型嵌入，并推导出流匹配与扩散采样的桥接方法，在酶设计基准上以更低的训练成本（682 GPU-hours）超越了Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于酶设计的全原子生成模型继承了结构预测的昂贵架构，训练成本高且样本多样性有限。作者认为，生成器仅依赖稀疏几何约束而非丰富的共进化信号，因此不需要如此复杂的架构。

Method: 提出Emyx：140M参数的条件流匹配模型。核心设计：(1) 将模型容量集中于标准Transformer块，使用轻量条件表示和稀疏连接替代重型嵌入栈；(2) 推导流匹配插值到EDM噪声级框架的精确重参数化，实现无需重训练即可桥接流匹配训练效率与扩散模型的最先进采样方法。

Result: 在AME酶设计基准上，Emyx在严格评估（要求全局折叠恢复和催化几何精度）下的成功率、结构新颖性、支架多样性和几何有效性方面均优于Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3，且仅用682 GPU小时训练，约为RFdiffusion3的1/4。

Conclusion: 酶设计生成模型无需继承结构预测的昂贵架构；轻量条件流匹配模型结合EDM噪声级重参数化，能以显著更低的计算成本实现更优的生成质量和多样性。

Abstract: Computational enzyme design requires generating proteins that scaffold catalytic residues and ligands, a task that demands both geometric accuracy and structural diversity from the underlying generative model. Current all-atom generators inherit expensive architectures from structure prediction, leading to high training costs and limited sample diversity. We argue that much of this complexity is unnecessary for generators, which condition on sparse geometric constraints rather than rich co-evolutionary signals. Emyx is a 140M-parameter conditional flow matching model that concentrates capacity within standard transformer blocks, replacing heavy embedding stacks with lightweight conditional representations and sparse connectivity. We additionally derive an exact reparametrisation of the flow matching interpolant into the EDM noise-level framework, bridging flow matching training efficiency with state-of-the-art sampling methods designed for diffusion models without retraining. Despite being the smallest model, Emyx outperforms both Proteína-Complexa and RFdiffusion3 against the AME enzyme design benchmark across success rate under strict evaluation requiring both global fold recovery and catalytic geometry accuracy, structural novelty, scaffold diversity, and geometric validity, while training in just $682$ GPU-hours, roughly $4\times$ less than RFdiffusion3.

</details>


### [269] [On the Oracle Complexity of Interpolation-Based Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19878)
*Dongmin Lee,William Lu,Anuran Makur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非精确梯度方法PPI-GD，通过在数据域等距采样并构造分段多项式插值来近似全梯度，在数据维度受限于样本数多对数函数时，该方法在强凸和非凸光滑损失函数下的oracle复杂度优于多种GD变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究指出ERM损失函数在训练数据上的光滑性（而非参数空间光滑性）可用于提升梯度下降的oracle复杂度。作者旨在利用这一洞察，设计一种通过数据域插值近似梯度的新方法，以获得更优的复杂度上界。

Method: 提出PPI-GD（Piecewise Polynomial Interpolation-based Gradient Descent）：每次迭代时在数据域等距点查询一阶oracle，将数据域划分成适当大小的块，在每个块上对梯度采样结果构造多项式插值来近似全梯度。分析中还将双三次样条插值的误差分析技术推广到d维张量积多项式插值。

Result: 当数据空间维度被样本数的多对数函数界定、且损失函数足够光滑时，PPI-GD在强凸和非凸情形下的oracle复杂度在关键区域优于多种GD变体。同时建立了d维张量积多项式插值的误差分析技术。

Conclusion: 利用数据域光滑性进行梯度插值近似是提升ERM优化效率的有效策略，PPI-GD在理论上实现了优于现有GD变体的复杂度，且其插值分析技术具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: Recent work on first-order optimizers for empirical risk minimization (ERM) has suggested that smoothness of ERM loss functions in the training data, rather than in the optimization parameters, can be leveraged to improve the oracle complexity of gradient descent (GD) methods. In this paper, we propose an inexact gradient method, piecewise polynomial interpolation-based gradient descent (PPI-GD), which approximates the full gradient in each iteration by querying the first-order oracle at equidistant points in the data domain to construct polynomial interpolants of the resulting gradient samples over appropriately sized patches of the data domain. We analyze the oracle complexity of PPI-GD for strongly convex and non-convex loss functions when the data space dimension is bounded by a polylogarithmic function of the number of training samples, and find it to outperform several GD variants in key regimes when the loss function is sufficiently smooth. Furthermore, our analysis extends several techniques from the error analysis of bicubic spline interpolants to the setting of $d$-variate tensor product polynomial interpolants which may be of independent interest in interpolation analysis.

</details>


### [270] [A Hybrid GNN-FEM Framework for Phase-Field Fracture Simulation. Physics-Preserving Hybridization for Generalizable Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19378)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Yongjin Choi,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合GNN-FEM框架用于相场断裂建模，用图神经网络替代交错方案中的相场更新步骤，同时保留FEM位移求解器以确保力学平衡。该框架通过无量纲特征设计、基于网格的图表示和物理信息损失函数，在几何、载荷、材料和离散化方面展现出强泛化能力，在保持精度的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场法虽为复杂裂纹演化提供了鲁棒的变分框架，但因其需要在增量有限元过程中求解耦合、非线性、历史依赖的系统，计算代价极高，限制了实际应用。现有纯数据驱动替代模型往往试图学习完整解轨迹，难以保证物理一致性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出混合GNN-FEM框架：(1)保留传统交错方案的增量求解结构，FEM位移求解器继续强制执行力学平衡和边界条件；(2)在每个载荷增量步中，用图神经网络(GNN)替代相场更新步骤；(3)通过无量纲特征设计、基于网格域的图构建公式、以及源自相场控制方程的物理信息损失函数，实现对不同几何、载荷、材料属性和离散化的泛化。

Result: 数值实验表明，该混合方法相比传统FEM降低了计算成本，同时保持了精度，并且在几何、加载条件、材料属性和网格离散化变化的多种问题设置下展现出鲁棒的预测性能。

Conclusion: 选择性替代策略通过聚焦物理上有意义且增量结构化的学习目标，而非依赖蛮力数据生成来学习完整断裂过程，为非线性、历史依赖系统的科学机器学习提供了一种高效且可泛化的新范式。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning (SciML) has emerged as a promising approach for accelerating simulations of complex physical systems, yet achieving physically consistent and generalizable predictions for nonlinear, history-dependent problems remains a central challenge. In this study, we propose a hybrid GNN--FEM framework for efficient and generalizable phase-field fracture modeling. While phase-field approaches provide a robust variational framework for simulating complex crack evolution, their high computational cost limits practical applications because they require solving coupled, nonlinear, and history-dependent systems within an incremental finite element procedure. To address this challenge, a graph neural network surrogate is integrated into the conventional staggered scheme, replacing the phase-field update at each load increment while retaining the FEM-based displacement solver to enforce mechanical equilibrium and boundary conditions. By preserving the incremental solution structure, the framework remains consistent with history-dependent fracture evolution without requiring the surrogate to approximate the full solution trajectory. This selective surrogate strategy emphasizes the identification of a physically meaningful and incrementally structured learning target, rather than relying on brute-force data generation to learn the full fracture process. The proposed framework achieves strong generalization across varying geometries, loading conditions, material properties, and discretizations through dimensionless feature design, a graph-based formulation on mesh-based domains, and a physics-informed loss derived from the governing phase-field equation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the hybrid approach reduces computational cost while maintaining accuracy compared with conventional FEM, and exhibits robust predictive performance across diverse problem settings.

</details>


### [271] [Matching Markets meet Cumulative Prospect Theory: Towards Optimal and Adversarially Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19883)
*Ananya Kunisetty,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有双边匹配市场的多智能体多臂老虎机问题，采用累积前景理论（CPT）建模人类偏好，提出改进的探索策略将遗憾界从 $\mathcal{O}(K\log T (1/\Delta)^{2/\alpha})$ 优化至与 $K$ 无关的最优遗憾，并进一步在对抗性市场中针对已知和未知腐败预算两种情形建立了鲁棒的对数级最优遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体多臂老虎机研究通常假设智能体完全理性，忽略了人类决策中的非线性风险偏好。CPT作为行为经济学核心理论能更真实地建模人类偏好，但其引入的$\alpha$-Hölder连续权重函数导致奖励失真，使得现有算法的遗憾分析面临挑战。此外，现实匹配市场存在对手恶意干扰（腐败奖励）的情形，需要鲁棒算法保障性能。

Method: 首先，在随机市场设定下，作者分析基于CPT权重失真奖励的UCB类算法，得到玩家最优遗憾界 $\mathcal{O}(K\log T (1/\Delta)^{2/\alpha})$。针对该项对最小偏好间隙$\Delta$的次优依赖，作者提出在探索阶段智能选择活跃臂集（active set of arms）的策略，使得主导项中消除$K$的依赖。在对抗性市场设定下，针对总腐败预算已知和未知两种情况，分别设计鲁棒算法，结合CPT作为风险敏感度量，通过对被污染奖励的鲁棒估计达成对数级遗憾界。

Result: （1）随机市场中，标准算法遗憾界为 $\mathcal{O}(K\log T (1/\Delta)^{2/\alpha})$；通过主动臂集选择策略改进后，当臂数$K$远大于玩家人数$N$时，主导项不再依赖$K$，获得了最优遗憾保证。（2）对抗性市场中，在腐败预算已知和未知两种情形下，所提鲁棒算法均建立了对数级的玩家最优遗憾界。

Conclusion: 将CPT引入多智能体多臂老虎机与双边匹配问题的交叉领域，成功刻画了人类非线性偏好下的学习动态。通过主动臂集选择策略解决了遗憾界对$K$的次优依赖问题，并首次在CPT框架下针对对抗性腐败市场建立了对数级最优遗憾保证，为行为经济学驱动的在线学习与匹配市场研究提供了理论基准。

Abstract: We study a multi-agent multi-armed bandit problem in the competitive setup with two-sided matching markets under a human centric decision making model. To capture human preferences, we use cumulative prospect theory (CPT) that weighs the actions of the agent in a nonlinear fashion using a ($α$-Hölder continuous) weight function. CPT has been widely used in behavioral economics and risk sensitive machine learning to emulate human preferences. We analyze the state-of-the-art learning algorithm with CPT weight distorted rewards and obtain a player optimal regret of $\mathcal{O}(K\log T \left(\frac{1}Δ\right)^{2/α})$, where $K$ denotes the number of arms, $T$ is the learning horizon, and $Δ$ represents (suitably defined) players' minimum preference gap. Noticing the dependence on $Δ$ to be sub-optimal, we further improve this regret by judiciously selecting the active set of arms during exploration, which removes the dependence on $K$ in the dominant term and achieves an improved (optimal) regret guarantees in the setting where the number of arms $K$ is significantly larger than the number of players $N$. In addition, we consider adversarial markets where the observed rewards of the agents may be corrupted. We propose and analyze algorithms for robust markets with CPT as risk sensitive measure in both settings where the total corruption budget is known and where it is unknown, and establish logarithmic player-optimal regret guarantees in both cases.

</details>


### [272] [How Linear Is a Transformer Feed-Forward Block? Per-Block Linear Recoverability Is Learned, Not Architectural](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19379)
*Stuart Whipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需优化器的度量指标R²_lin（线性可恢复性），用于量化Transformer中FFN块的非线性程度。研究发现FFN块的线性度在不同模型和层级间高度异质，这是训练中学习到的特性而非架构决定。该度量还可作为压缩信号和线性基线方法的校准工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的前馈网络（FFN）通常被视为计算的非线性存储单元，但训练后的FFN块实际有多"非线性"却很少被精确测量。现有研究缺乏一个统一的、无需优化的指标来量化单个FFN块的线性程度。

Method: 将每个FFN视为位置级输入到输出的映射，将其分解为精确的最小二乘线性近似加上残差。线性映射解释的保留方差定义为该块的线性可恢复性R²_lin——一个闭合形式的、无需优化器的线性度量。此外，使用低秩双线性探针对残差进行进一步分析。

Result: 在GPT-2、Pythia-160m和Llama-160m的全部12个块上，R²_lin高度异质且随深度非单调变化，范围从接近线性（>0.99）到强非线性（<0.3），相邻块之间差异巨大。该特性不由激活函数决定（同为GELU的GPT-2和Pythia-160m表现出截然不同的剖面）。低秩双线性探针仅能恢复残差中的少量R²，增益与残差非线性不相关，表明未恢复的计算是高阶或分布式结构。高可恢复性块允许大幅单层替换（GPT-2早期FFN仅用1/8参数，perplexity仅增加0.77）。研究还揭示了训练线性基线在病态条件transformer激活上可能严重欠收敛的方法论陷阱。

Conclusion: R²_lin是一个有效且实用的FFN线性度度量工具，可用于指导模型压缩、识别不适合线性化的块，并为线性探针等分析方法提供精确的闭合形式理论上限，避免因优化不足导致的错误结论。

Abstract: Transformer feed-forward networks (FFNs) are often treated as nonlinear stores of computation, yet how nonlinear a trained FFN block actually is has rarely been measured. We treat each FFN as a position-wise input-to-output map and split it into the exact least-squares linear approximation plus a residual. The held-out variance the closed-form linear map explains defines a block's linear recoverability (R^2_lin), an optimiser-free measure of its linearity.
  Across all twelve blocks of GPT-2, Pythia-160m, and llama-160m, R^2_lin is highly heterogeneous and non-monotone with depth, ranging from near-linear (>0.99) to strongly nonlinear (<0.3) between adjacent blocks, and is not set by the activation function: same-width GELU models GPT-2 and Pythia-160m have sharply different profiles, so recoverability is a learned property of individual trained blocks, not an architectural one. A low-rank bilinear probe of the residual recovers only a few points of R^2, with gain uncorrelated with residual nonlinearity: the unrecovered computation is not a single position-wise product but higher-order or distributed structure.
  The measurement also serves as a targeted compression signal: recoverable blocks admit large single-layer replacements (GPT-2's early FFN at 8x fewer parameters for +0.77 perplexity), while low-recoverability blocks flag where this is unsafe. It further exposes a methodological pitfall: trained linear baselines can badly under-converge on ill-conditioned transformer activations, so we report the exact closed-form least-squares ceiling throughout.

</details>


### [273] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多校准（multicalibration）领域的一个核心开放问题：证明了确定性预测器可以达到极小极大最优的样本复杂度，推翻了此前认为随机化是必要的猜想，并将结果推广到结果不可区分性（OI）、全预测器（omnipredictors）和泛预测器（panpredictors）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多校准要求预测器不仅在整体上校准（即条件于预测值无偏），而且在按任意群组权重重加权后仍保持校准，这是可信机器学习的基本要求。此前达到极小极大最优样本复杂度 Õ(ε⁻³) 的算法均为随机化预测器，确定性预测器的样本复杂度远差于此。[CLNR26] 明确提问：随机化是否为多校准最优样本复杂度的必要条件？本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 作者给出了首个输出确定性预测器且达到极小极大最优样本复杂度的多校准算法。算法核心思想在于构造性地证明了确定性预测器同样可以达到 Õ(ε⁻³) 的速率，无需借助随机化。在此基础上，将算法推广至有限或有限覆盖检验族下的结果不可区分性（Outcome Indistinguishability, OI），进而得到最优样本复杂度的确定性全预测器和泛预测器。

Result: （1）给出了极小极大最优的确定性多校准算法，解决了[CLNR26]提出的开放问题；（2）推广到OI场景，得到有限/有限覆盖检验族下的最优确定性预测器；（3）作为应用，给出了具有最优样本复杂度的确定性全预测器和泛预测器，解决了[OKK25]和[BHHLZ25]提出的开放问题。

Conclusion: 随机化并非多校准最优样本复杂度的必要条件，确定性预测器同样可以达到 Õ(ε⁻³) 的极小极大最优速率。这一结论及其在OI、全预测器和泛预测器上的推广，奠定了确定性算法在该领域的理论基础。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


### [274] [VERITAS: Verifier-Guided Proof Search for Zero-Shot Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19399)
*Manish Acharya,Zhenyu Liao,Yueke Zhang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERITAS 提出一种零样本框架，将验证器的丰富反馈信号（语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）通过两阶段协议（先 Best-of-N 采样，再基于 Phase 1 失败样本作为负例的 MCTS 搜索）路由回证明搜索过程，在 miniF2F 上达到 40.6%，并发布了组合数学基准 VERITAS-CombiBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的形式化证明器通常将验证器产生的丰富信号（如语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）粗暴压缩为二元的通过/失败比特，大量有用的中间反馈信息被丢弃，导致证明搜索效率低下，尤其在需要从验证器反馈中迭代恢复正确引理名称的场景中表现不佳。

Method: 提出 VERITAS 框架，采用两阶段协议：(1) Phase 1 执行 Best-of-N 采样，记录所有失败样本；(2) Phase 2 将 Phase 1 的失败样本作为显式负例，输入到 critic 引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）中，使验证器的每一条反馈信号都能路由回证明搜索过程。Phase 2 增加的证明均可归因于反馈驱动的探索。

Result: 在 miniF2F 上达到 40.6%（独立运行的 Best-of-5 为 36.9%，Portfolio 为 26.2%）；在作者发布的 55 道组合数学基准 VERITAS-CombiBench 上达到 7.3%，而 Best-of-5 仅 1.8%、Portfolio 为 3.6%，表明在需要从验证器反馈中迭代恢复正确引理名称时，无引导采样反而有害。

Conclusion: 将验证器的全谱反馈信号纳入证明搜索可显著提升 LLM 形式化证明的性能，反馈驱动的 MCTS 探索在组合数学等需要精确引理选择的场景中尤为关键。VERITAS 的协议设计保证了 Phase 1 已解出定理不被丢失，增量收益可清晰归因。

Abstract: LLM-based formal provers often collapse rich verifier signals (syntax errors, type mismatches, partial goal progress) into a binary pass/fail bit. We present VERITAS, a zero-shot framework that routes every verifier signal back into proof search through a two-phase protocol: Best-of-N sampling first, then a critic-guided MCTS pass that ingests Phase 1 failures as explicit negative examples. The protocol preserves every theorem solved by its own Phase 1 sweep, so Phase 2's additional solves are attributable to feedback-driven exploration. VERITAS reaches 40.6% on miniF2F (vs. an independently run Best-of-5 at 36.9%, Portfolio 26.2%) and 7.3% on VERITAS-CombiBench, a 55-theorem combinatorics benchmark we release on which Best-of-5 (1.8%) falls below Portfolio (3.6%), exposing that unguided sampling hurts when correct lemma names must be recovered iteratively from verifier feedback. Artifacts are available on GitHub.

</details>


### [275] [Thermodynamic Signatures of Reasoning: Free-Energy and Spectral-Form-Factor Diagnostics for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19404)
*Salim Khazem*

Main category: cs.LG
